We propose a reliable tree recruitment framework that expands a small annotated inventory by iteratively self-training a multi-task DeepLabV3 network, combining semantic segmentation with a crown-distance regression head, on RGB UAV orthomosaics. At each iteration, candidate crowns enter the training inventory only if they pass a confidence filter on class probability and distance map and a morphology filter on crown area and solidity, with at most five new crowns per species, keeping recruited samples consistent with the reference inventory. We evaluate the framework at two Brazilian sites: an Amazonian rainforest in Acre (17 species, 355 annotated crowns, 4 cm GSD) and a forest site in the Cerrado of Maranhão (4 species, 51 annotated crowns, 1.8 cm GSD). Over 20 iterations, the number of retained crowns grew from about 500 to 960 in Acre and from about 700 to over 3100 in Maranhão, while crown-level F1 exceeded single- and multi-task DeepLabV3 baselines (53.5% vs. 50.0% and 45.2%; 92.2% vs. 79.7% and 83.6%). The framework offers a low-cost route to expanding species inventories with consumer-grade UAVs and limited fieldwork.

</details>


### [138] [RAPID: Reconstruction Accelerated by Polarization-Informed Depth for 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24404)
*Daisong Ouyang,Linlin Xia,Liangkun Yan,Song Gu,Shedong Zhao,Xin Dai,Yi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAPID：利用多视图偏振线索获取稠密表面先验的"几何优先"高斯泼溅框架，实现约5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图3D表面重建中，现有高斯泼溅方法依赖稀疏几何初始化与迭代致密化，导致优化开销大，且难以在反射与弱纹理区域平衡渲染质量与几何精度。

Method: 用多视图偏振线索恢复稠密表面先验：借助稀疏几何锚点将表面法向融入深度图，并进行跨视图深度融合；随后采用曲率自适应采样与有向高斯初始化以支持高效优化。

Result: 在PISR数据集上相比SOTA方法PhyGaP约5倍加速，平均优化时间从约21分钟降至4分钟，同时保持有竞争力的新视图合成质量与网格重建精度。

Conclusion: RAPID通过偏振多视图输入的稠密几何先验有效加速高斯优化，验证了几何优先策略在效率与精度平衡上的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient and accurate 3D surface reconstruction from multi-view images remains a fundamental challenge in Gaussian splatting. Many existing methods rely on sparse geometric initialization and iterative densification, resulting in substantial optimization overhead and difficulties in balancing rendering quality and geometric accuracy, particularly in reflective and weakly textured regions. To address these limitations, we propose RAPID, a geometry-first framework that uses multiview polarization cues to recover dense surface priors. We integrate surface normals into depth maps using sparse geometric anchors. These depth maps are then fused across views. Curvature-adaptive sampling and oriented Gaussian initialization then support efficient optimization. Experiments on the PISR dataset demonstrate an approximately \(5\times\) speedup over PhyGaP, which is a state-of-the-art Gaussian-based reconstruction method. RAPID reduces the average optimization time from approximately 21 minutes (PhyGaP) to 4 minutes while maintaining competitive novel-view synthesis quality and mesh reconstruction accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of RAPID in accelerating Gaussian optimization from polarimetric multi-view inputs.

</details>


### [139] [StrideWorld: Benchmarking Scene Rigidity and Action Consistency in Interactive Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24408)
*Chuang Wang,Rui Nie,Jie Song,Junyuan Gao,Jiahe Song,Haitao Zhou,Boyu Zhu,Xing Li,Jiang Wu,Conghui He,Qian Yu,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 StrideWorld 基准，用运动前固定的参考来评估交互式视频世界模型的场景刚性与动作一致性，揭示多数模型存在场景形变与路径偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型基准的参考（自重建场景、自由尺度的对齐轨迹）是依被测视频拟合的，会吸收渐进的场景形变与节奏、路径长度变化，导致此类错误无法被检测。

Method: 参考在运动被评估之前即固定：场景刚性以起始视图的真值几何为参考；动作一致性以每条指令对应的"步幅"（模型最初几步测得的相机运动）为参考，再用后续步骤检验，实现模型自比。测量在已知相机运动的渲染视频上验证，并与人类判断比对。

Result: 在 140 个起始视图、多个开源模型生成的视频上，除一个模型外全部出现场景形变，偏移最高达刚性渲染的 7.3 倍；单一动作保持时节奏较稳定；组合路径上多模型停滞，多数完成路径的终点偏离按其自身步幅预测的位置，仅两个按估计场景深度缩放指令的模型表现较好；少量渲染片段微调可消除大部分中途停滞。

Conclusion: 提出一种参考先固定、模型自比的评测范式，能暴露此前被吸收的形变与节奏误差；深度缩放指令与轻量微调是有效改进方向。

Abstract: Interactive video world models turn user commands into the exploration of a generated scene, which requires the scene to keep its 3D structure as the camera moves and each action to keep a stable effect. Existing benchmarks mostly evaluate these properties against references fitted to the video under test, either a scene reconstructed from its own frames or a camera trajectory aligned to the command path with a free scale. Such references can absorb gradual scene deformation and changes in pace and route length, so these errors can pass undetected. We therefore introduce StrideWorld, a benchmark whose references are fixed before the motion they judge. It evaluates scene rigidity against the ground-truth geometry of the start view, and action consistency against the stride of each action, the camera motion of one command. The stride is measured on the model's first steps and then used to test the steps that follow, so each model is judged only against its own steps. Its measurements are validated on rendered videos with known camera motion, and its scores agree with human judgments. Across \nvideos{} videos generated by \nmodels{} open models from $140$ start views, all models but one deform their scenes as the camera moves, with offsets up to $7.3$ times those of a rigid rendering along the same camera path. Most models keep a steady pace when a single action is held. Along composed routes, several models often stall, and most completed routes end away from the positions predicted by each model's own strides. Only the two models that scale commands by the estimated scene depth bring most routes close to these positions. Fine-tuning two models on a few rendered clips removes most of their mid-route stalls. Code, data, and a leaderboard are publicly available at https://github.com/chuangwang123/strideworld.

</details>


### [140] [Why Multi-Source Audio Fails to Compose: A Geometric Diagnosis and Its Remedy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24410)
*Anurag Singh,Prajwal Singh,Shanmuganathan Raman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"几何位移"诊断框架，揭示多源音频到图像生成中音频token在CLIP文本空间位置偏移、语义无锚定导致的组合性失败，并用多token占位符映射+混合推理目标在无需重训练扩散骨干的情况下显著提升组合忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源音频驱动的图像生成存在一致的组合性失败：音频token在CLIP文本token空间中占据几何位移、语义未锚定的位置，扩散U-Net缺乏可靠关注并将其定位到不同空间区域的先验。作者希望形式化并诊断这一"几何位移"现象，并据此改进生成。

Method: 1) 通过两级聚类分析（原始编码器层与投影后CLIP token层）比较BEATs与CLAP嵌入，形式化几何位移现象；2) 提炼三项关键几何性质：类内紧致性、与CLIP文本流形的角度对齐、token间空间分离度；3) 提出无需重训练扩散骨干的多token扩展：为每个音频源分配CLIP tokenizer中独立的占位token；4) 推理时混合目标：将Attend-and-Excite与A*空间分离损失结合，缓解残余跨token干扰；5) 从VGGSound构建多音频组合基准（VGG2、VGG3、VGGN），并将六项标准音频-图像指标扩展到多源设定。

Result: CLAP因对比式语言-音频预训练而与自然语言语义对齐，在三项几何性质上均显著优于BEATs，silhouette分数提升超3倍，Audio-Image Content（AIC）分数亦提升。在五个基准与四个强基线对比中，方法在组合忠实度相关指标上表现最佳：AIC较最强基线最高提升2.25倍，双源生成FID降低近2倍。

Conclusion: 几何位移可作为跨模态生成系统的诊断框架，适用于将学习到的嵌入投影到预训练对比空间的场景；所提分析与多token方案在不重训练扩散骨干前提下有效提升多源音频-图像生成的组合忠实度。

Abstract: Multi-source audio-to-image generation exhibits consistent compositional failures when audio tokens occupy geometrically displaced, semantically unanchored positions in the CLIP text-token space, where the diffusion U-Net lacks the prior to reliably attend to them or ground them in distinct spatial regions. We formalize this as the geometric displacement phenomenon and expose it through a two-level clustering analysis comparing BEATs and CLAP embeddings at the raw encoder and projected CLIP token levels. Three geometric properties emerge as important: intra-class compactness, angular alignment with the CLIP text manifold, and inter-token spatial separation. We show that CLAP, whose contrastive language-audio pretraining aligns embeddings with natural language semantics, substantially outperforms BEATs on all three properties, improving the silhouette score by more than 3x and the Audio-Image Content score as well. Building on this analysis, we introduce a multi-token extension that requires no diffusion-backbone retraining and assigns each audio source to a distinct placeholder token in the CLIP tokenizer, complemented by a mixed inference-time objective that combines Attend-and-Excite with an A* spatial separation loss to resolve residual cross-token interference. For evaluation, we construct multi-audio compositional benchmarks from VGGSound (VGG2, VGG3, and VGGN) and extend six standard audio-image metrics to the multi-source setting. Across five benchmarks and four strong baselines, our method achieves strong results on the metrics most directly tied to compositional faithfulness, with AIC improvements of up to 2.25x over the strongest baseline and FID reduced by nearly 2x on two-source generation. We propose geometric displacement as a diagnostic framework for cross-modal generation systems that project learned embeddings into a pretrained contrastive space.

</details>


### [141] [PhysResponse: Probing the Physics of Video Generation with Controlled Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24419)
*Yuhan Li,Shuran Zhang,Haixin Tian,Baonuo Rao,Weipeng Li,Yujia Hou,Zhenyu Wang,Zijun Wang,Luoyang Lin,Lijing Luo,Jiehui Huang,Kaiyuan Yang,Jingbin Tan,Panwen Hu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysResponse基准，从"场景条件下的模拟器反演"和"临界转变事件"两个层面衡量视频生成模型对物理参数（重力、摩擦、初速）的响应能力，并构建7.2万条受控仿真视频提供训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型被视为世界模拟器，需对指定物理参数作出正确动力学响应；但现有模型、数据集与基准多只关注"物理合理性"，对"物理参数响应性"探索不足。

Method: 构建双层基准：①场景条件下的模拟器反演，从生成运动中恢复有效重力、摩擦系数与初始速度；②事件评估，测试临界转变附近及跨越转变的结局。评估10个图生视频模型。另构建72,000条覆盖20类多物体交互、参数受控的仿真视频用于微调，并进一步引入数值编码器继续训练。

Result: 最低参数误差仍比物理正确视频高一个数量级以上；模型很少同时复现转变对的两个结局；两层暴露互补性失败，且PSNR等视觉指标无法捕捉。微调后参数误差下降，并可泛化到未见交互类型、真实视频及外部物理基准；加入数值编码器继续训练后，留出类型上三项误差均进一步降低。

Conclusion: PhysResponse为"遵循物理条件而非仅外观"的世界模拟器研究提供基准与训练数据基础。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly viewed as world simulators, which requires not only plausible motion but dynamics that respond correctly to specified physical parameters. Existing models, datasets and benchmarks, however, largely target physical plausibility, leaving this physical parameter responsiveness insufficiently explored. We introduce PhysResponse, a benchmark that measures it at two levels: scene-conditioned simulator inversion recovers the effective gravity, friction and initial speed of generated motion, and event evaluation tests outcomes near and across critical transitions. Evaluating ten image-to-video models, we find that even the lowest parameter errors exceed those of physically correct videos by over an order of magnitude, that models rarely reproduce both outcomes of a transition pair, and that the two levels expose complementary failures that visual metrics such as PSNR miss. To supply the missing training signal, we construct 72,000 simulated videos of 20 multi-object interaction types with controlled parameters; fine-tuning on them lowers parameter errors, even on held-out interaction types, and transfers to real videos and an external physics benchmark. Continued training with a numerical encoder further lowers all three errors on held-out types. PhysResponse provides a foundation for world simulators that follow physical conditions, not just appearances.

</details>


### [142] [Let a Small Model Read First: Faster Prefill for Long Interleaved Image-Text Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24431)
*Xiaolin Lin,Kai-Chiang Wu,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkimPrefill 利用小型混合架构模型（Qwen3.5-0.8B）早期层的一个注意力头，以半分辨率读入长交错输入并据此对图像/段落等“单元”打分，为目标大模型筛选前 10% 单元作为精简提示，实现 3.7–11.6 倍 prefill 加速且保持平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）处理约 100K token 的长交错输入时，首个输出 token 的延迟主要由 prefill 主导。现有剪枝方法需用目标模型自身或同族小模型先写出答案再对 token 打分，这一打分过程耗时几乎抵消了剪枝收益；因此需要一种廉价的证据定位方式。

Method: 关键观察：（1）回答问题所需的证据集中在少数完整单元（整张图像或整段文字）中；（2）小型混合模型仅凭早期层的一个注意力头即可定位这些单元，即使图像为半分辨率。据此提出 SkimPrefill：先让小型混合模型（Qwen3.5-0.8B）以半分辨率阅读完整输入至该早期层（其多数层为线性复杂度，故该次前向很短），再用该注意力头对每个单元排名，取 Top 单元拼成常规较短提示交给目标模型。该选择器无需训练，可跨模型族通用。

Result: 在 MMLongBench（128K token、5 类任务、19 个数据集）上，保留 10% 输入 token 时，SkimPrefill 使 Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-12B、Qwen3-VL-32B 的 prefill 分别加速 3.7×、11.6×、8.2×，并维持其平均准确率。

Conclusion: 以“整单元”为粒度的粗读式预填充（skim-prefill）优于 token 级打分式剪枝：借助小混合模型的早期层注意力头即可低成本定位证据，且免训练选择器对多族目标模型均有效，在长上下文多模态场景下显著降低首 token 延迟而不牺牲精度。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer questions about long interleaved inputs, such as documents of many pages and collections of hundreds of images, but they are slow to start: on inputs of about 100K tokens, the prefill takes most of the wait for the first output token. A common approach prunes the input by scoring its tokens with the target model itself or with a small model of the same family that first writes an answer. However, this scoring takes much of the time that pruning saves. We find that the evidence for a question lies in a few whole units (images or paragraphs), and that a small hybrid model finds them with one attention head of an early layer, even at half image resolution. Based on these findings, we propose SkimPrefill, which lets the small hybrid model read the whole input first, up to that early layer, with images at half resolution. Most layers of the small hybrid model (Qwen3.5-0.8B) run in linear time, so this pass is short. SkimPrefill then ranks every unit with that head and gives the target the top units as an ordinary shorter prompt. One untrained selector serves targets of different model families. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench at 128K tokens, with 19 datasets of five task types, show the effectiveness of SkimPrefill. With 10% of the input tokens kept, SkimPrefill speeds up the prefill of Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-12B and Qwen3-VL-32B by 3.7 times, 11.6 time and 8.2 times while maintaining their average accuracy.

</details>


### [143] [MLIE-CoT: Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Multi-Level Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24433)
*Baichuan Yang,Dongyuan Xu,Zhao Yang,Yong Liu,Bing Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MLIE-CoT，一种基于多级图像编辑的视觉思维链框架，按错误严重程度选择三种修复粒度，减少整图重生成带来的幻觉累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成的 CoT 主要依赖文本推理，无法充分利用和保留图像中已有的正确语义信息（语言难以穷尽所有视觉细节）；而将图像作为上下文前向传递的方法又受两个问题困扰：不合适的前序图像会强烈干扰模型，且每轮整图重新生成会引入并累积新的幻觉。

Method: MLIE-CoT 在多级图像编辑基础上学习从三个层级中选择修复粒度，在推理过程中尽可能保留正确语义并抑制错误内容的影响；此外构建了覆盖不同错误情况的图像修复数据集，用于训练修复层级路由器（repair-level router），使多模态模型能把不同严重程度的错误路由到相应修复层级。

Result: 大量实验表明，MLIE-CoT 显著优于其基座模型以及采用固定修复策略的方法，在多个基准上均取得明显提升。

Conclusion: 以多级图像编辑替代整图重生成的视觉思维链是可行且有效的，自适应修复粒度选择能兼顾语义保留与幻觉抑制，为视觉生成模型的复杂任务推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has proven effective in improving the reasoning abilities of large language models by breaking complex tasks into step-by-step solutions. In recent years, researchers have attempted to extend CoT reasoning to visual generative models to improve their ability to handle complex tasks. However, existing CoT methods for text-to-image generation still rely mainly on textual reasoning and therefore cannot fully use and preserve the correct semantic information already present in an image, as even the most detailed language cannot capture all visual information. With advances in image generation, models such as FLUX and BAGEL can generate images conditioned on visual inputs. Recent work has attempted to carry images forward as context in the chain of thought, but this introduces another limitation: unsuitable earlier images can strongly interfere with the model, while repeated whole-image regeneration introduces new hallucinations at each round and causes them to accumulate. To address these issues, we propose MLIE-CoT, a visual chain-of-thought framework based on multi-level image editing. MLIE-CoT learns to select the repair granularity from three levels, preserving correct semantics whenever possible during reasoning while reducing the effect of incorrect content. To improve the ability of multimodal models to route errors of different severity to appropriate repair levels, we construct an image-repair dataset covering different error cases and use it to train a repair-level router. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MLIE-CoT significantly outperforms both its base models and methods using fixed repair strategies, producing clear improvements across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [144] [Explainable and CPU-Efficient Brain Tumor Classification from MRI Using Two-Phase EfficientNetB3 Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24440)
*Deepro Das,Saad Kabir Uddin,Mohammad Alauddin,Bornali Sarker,Thasnim Akhter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于EfficientNetB3迁移学习与自定义分类头的两阶段训练流程，实现四类脑部MRI自动分类（胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、无肿瘤、垂体瘤），在1600张平衡测试集上达90.62%准确率与0.9854宏平均AUC，并集成Grad-CAM提供可解释性，兼顾CPU端效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤为高致死性神经系统疾病，全球年新发病例超30.8万；MRI肿瘤分型对治疗方案制定至关重要，但人工判读耗时且存在观察者间差异。既有工作难以同时兼顾高精度、CPU级算力效率与临床可解释性三个维度。

Method: 采用EfficientNetB3迁移学习+自定义分类头；两阶段训练：阶段一冻结骨干仅训练分类头，阶段二以1/100学习率选择性微调顶部37层；引入Grad-CAM生成类别判别性热力图进行可视化解释。

Result: 阶段一最佳验证准确率92.77%，阶段二最佳验证准确率94.91%；在独立平衡测试集（1600张MRI）上总体准确率90.62%，宏平均AUC 0.9854。

Conclusion: 两阶段迁移学习结合选择性微调可在仅CPU条件下获得较高分类精度，Grad-CAM增强临床可信度，方法在精度、算力效率与可解释性三方面同时取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Brain tumors are among the most lethal neurological disorders, with over 308,000 new diagnoses annually worldwide. Accurate classification of tumor type from Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) scans is critical for treatment planning; however, manual radiological interpretation is time-consuming and prone to inter-observer variability. This paper proposes an automated deep learning pipeline for four-class brain MRI classification (glioma, meningioma, no tumor, and pituitary tumor) using EfficientNetB3 transfer learning with a custom classification head. A two-phase training strategy is adopted: Phase 1 trains the head with a frozen backbone (best validation accuracy: 92.77%), and Phase 2 selectively fine-tunes the top 37 layers at a 100x reduced learning rate (best validation accuracy: 94.91%). On an independent balanced test set of 1,600 MRI images, the system achieves 90.62% overall accuracy and a macro-average AUC of 0.9854. Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) is integrated for class-discriminative visual explainability. The proposed system simultaneously addresses three dimensions absent in prior work: high accuracy, CPU-only computational efficiency, and clinical interpretability.

</details>


### [145] [NativeSeg: Native Vision Language Segmentation of 3D Computed Tomography via Spatial Visual Tokens and Verified Local Zoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24443)
*Chengqian Ma,Gaole Dai,Zijie Meng,Weixuan Jin,Yijie Du,Yuexiao Ma,Xiawu Zheng,Fei Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NativeSeg 在 LoRA 微调的 Qwen3.8-27B 上直接于 VLM 的 2D 空间 token 上完成稠密掩码解码与多轮局部放大，无需外部 SAM 解码器，实现原生文本提示 3D CT 分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本提示 3D CT 分割需同时具备全层面上下文与精细边界精度，且超过 80% 的被提示层面不含目标器官。现有医学 VLM 多为"非原生"分割器：将像素描绘外包给外部 SAM 解码器（1D [SEG] 向量）、用有损离散码本量化掩码，或向无状态外部 SAM 发出框提示，缺乏空层面筛查与空间状态记忆。

Method: 在 LoRA 适配的 Qwen3.8-27B 上进行稠密掩码解码、体数据分诊、空间误差自诊断与多轮局部放大；由上层 2D 空间 token 与五切层 U-Net 通路融合解码置信图；原生存在门控 <pres> 在独立评估中于 >91% 情况下零放大即停止；20 单元多尺度误差门控 <err> 定位不确定边界；仅当预测误差下降时接受放大更新。

Result: 仅用成人 CT 训练，Round 0 DSC 相较离散码本与 1D [SEG] 读出在相同骨干上分别提升 +19.81 与 +7.04 点；成人 DSC 达 86.81%，零样本儿童达 87.04%；可安全精化 2D nnU-Net（成人 89.52%、儿童 86.86%）、3D SegVol（86.81%）与 3D VoxTell（89.85%）。

Conclusion: 原生 VLM 内置空间解码与自诊断放大机制可在不依赖外部 SAM 的情况下显著提升分割精度，并具备跨人群零样本泛化与对现有 2D/3D 分割器的安全精化能力。

Abstract: Text prompted 3D computed tomography (CT) segmentation requires both full slice context and fine boundary precision across volumes where over $80\%$ of organ prompted slices lack the queried target. Existing medical vision language models (VLMs) are largely non native segmenters: they delegate pixel delineation outside the VLM by compressing reasoning into a 1D [SEG] vector for an external Segment Anything Model (SAM) decoder, quantizing masks into lossy discrete codebooks, or issuing box prompts to stateless external SAM tools without empty slice screening or spatial state memory. We introduce NativeSeg, a native vision language segmentation framework on Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapted Qwen3.8-27B that performs dense mask decoding, volumetric triage, spatial error self diagnosis, and multiround local zoom directly over the VLM's 2D spatial visual tokens without external SAM decoders. NativeSeg decodes confidence maps from upper layer 2D spatial tokens fused with a five slice U-Net pathway, halts at zero zooms on $>91\%$ of standalone evaluations via a native presence gate (<pres>), localizes uncertain boundaries via a 20 cell multiscale error gate (<err>), and accepts zoomed updates only when predicted error decreases. Trained solely on adult CT, NativeSeg improves Round $0$ DSC by $+19.81$ and $+7.04$ points over discrete codebook and 1D [SEG] readouts on the identical backbone, reaches $86.81\%$ adult and $87.04\%$ zero shot pediatric DSC, and safely refines 2D nnU-Net ($89.52\%$ adult; $86.86\%$ pediatric), 3D SegVol ($86.81\%$), and 3D VoxTell ($89.85\%$).

</details>


### [146] [Retroactive Concept Bottleneck Models for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24445)
*Yang Ji,Ying Sun,Yuanxin Zhuang,Yuting Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RetroCBM：无需样本的类增量学习框架，通过保存少量与词表无关的特征统计量，实现新概念的追溯监督与旧类预测的再校准，在六个数据集上优于现有可解释方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续概念瓶颈模型（CBM）主要致力于保留已学概念或用生成特征近似旧类，导致新学概念在旧类预测中未被充分利用，限制了可解释增量学习的性能。

Method: 冻结视觉特征与预训练视觉-语言模型嵌入空间；保存其关联的少量词表无关统计量；提出追溯回归规则，使每个新概念的预测器等价于对迄今所有图像做类平衡岭回归的结果；再投影统计量以恢复全历史平方分类损失，学习新概念对旧类预测的贡献权重；并引入图加权惩罚，约束由系统性概念错误引起的间隔偏移。

Result: 在六个数据集的各类类增量设置中均优于最先进的可解释方法，平均增量准确率相较最强无样本基线取得18.1%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过统计量保留替代样本回放，可实现新概念对旧类预测的追溯性利用，为无样本可解释类增量学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) requires models to learn new classes sequentially without retaining training samples from previous tasks. Continual concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make their predictions interpretable by identifying visual concepts in an image and using them to score each class. However, these methods typically focus on preserving learned concepts or approximating past classes with generated features, which can leave new concepts underused in old-class predictions. We propose RetroCBM, which retains a small set of vocabulary-independent statistics relating frozen visual features to the embedding space of a pretrained vision-language model. Even after earlier training images have been discarded, we can use these statistics to supervise new concepts. To do so, we develop a retroactive regression rule that yields the same predictor for each new concept as class-balanced ridge regression on all images seen so far. We then reproject the stored statistics to recover the full-history squared classification loss and thus learn how much each new concept should contribute to old-class predictions. Since the classifier may use systematic concept errors to distinguish nearby classes, we further introduce a graph-weighted penalty on the margin shifts caused by those errors. Across six datasets, RetroCBM outperforms state-of-the-art interpretable methods in all class-incremental settings, with an 18.1\% relative gain in mean average incremental accuracy over the strongest exemplar-free baseline.

</details>


### [147] [EviScope: Evidence-Conditioned Sampling for Long-Range 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24451)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Gongce Cao,Chujie Tang,Yuhao Zhang,Lu Jia,Qian Zhang,Bo Wang,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScope 用非学习的鸟瞰雷达证据场显式调节查询的径向采样范围与八帧权重，仅加 5,450 参数，但与同参数量几何对照相比增益不稳定，主效应不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在 MAN TruckScenes 150 m 远距场景中，查询级雷达回波数量是否应参与决定"采样哪里、采样哪些帧"，即雷达证据能否作为查询的采样分配依据。

Method: 以当前帧雷达回波构建非学习鸟瞰证据场，查询在其解码中心读取该场，用于缩放径向采样范围并以八帧加权；作为 RaCFormer 的轻量插件（+0.009% 参数，无明显延迟）；设置同参数量几何条件器对照与匹配种子/端到端比较。

Result: 无模块配方在七次运行中 mAP 25.9 超过已发表 24.1；查询读到的证据随目标回波数上升（Spearman 0.51），学习半径与证据强负相关（ρ≈−0.91）。七组四轮配对中模块使 NDS +0.31±0.36、mAP −0.27±0.22；对同参数量几何对照则 +1.25±0.72 NDS、+0.87±0.21 mAP（mAP 每对均更优）。

Conclusion: 查询局部雷达证据的收益有限且不稳健；探索性远距增益未通过预设留出检验，推理期替换惩罚在无查询局部证据时同样出现，说明学习到的分配或推理期依赖都不能替代匹配的端到端比较。

Abstract: Query-based camera-radar detectors choose where and over which frames each object query samples from the query's position or feature. We test whether radar evidence around the query should also set these choices at the 150 m range of MAN TruckScenes, where recall differs across object-level radar-return counts within every range band. EviScope is a minimal test: the current frame's returns form a non-learned bird's-eye evidence field, which each query reads at its decoded centre to scale its radial sampling extent and to weight eight frames. It adds 5,450 parameters (0.009%) to RaCFormer, with no latency increase in our timing. Our module-free recipe exceeds the published TruckScenes mAP (25.9 over seven runs against 24.1), so we measure the module only against its own baseline at matched seeds. The evidence a query reads rises with its object's return count (Spearman 0.51) but captures zero-return objects only in part, and the learned radius tracks it ($ρ\approx-0.91$ in four runs). Over seven four-epoch pairs the module changes NDS by $+0.31\pm0.36$ and mAP by $-0.27\pm0.22$ (mean $\pm$ SE). Against a geometry-only conditioner with the same parameter count, trained at three matched seeds, it is higher by $+1.25\pm0.72$ NDS and $+0.87\pm0.21$ mAP, on mAP in every pair, and the control's mean NDS is near the baseline's. An exploratory far-range gain failed an internally pre-specified held-out test, and large inference-time substitution penalties also occur without query-local evidence: neither learned allocation nor inference-time dependence substitutes for matched end-to-end comparisons.

</details>


### [148] [ShoWorld: Shape-Faithful Multi-Hypothesis Occupancy World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24457)
*Zhiqiang Yan,Guangfa Wang,Yuan Wu,Jian Yang,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShoWorld通过将运动与形状解耦表示，解决占据世界模型中的运动不确定性与形状畸变问题，在Occ3D-nuScenes上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 占据世界模型在预测运动物体时面临两大难题：一是运动不确定性，单一轨迹会把多个可能的未来平均成不合理的路径；二是形状畸变，即使运动正确，重复体素化也会侵蚀或膨胀物体形状。

Method: 提出运动与形状分离的表示。运动预测器以场景布局、邻近智能体和未来自车轨迹为条件，预测概率化轨迹；每个假设渲染为占据并按概率融合，以保留多种未来。形状部分从局部几何预测带占据概率和偏移的亚体素点；这些点随物体刚性运动，体素占据由“至少一个占据点落入该体素”的概率决定。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上达到SOTA。在给定真值占据和自车轨迹条件下，整体mIoU和IoU分别提升6.56和9.16点，动态类别上分别提升6.06和5.53点。

Conclusion: 将运动与形状解耦、并以概率融合多轨迹、以亚体素点刚性承载形状，可有效缓解运动不确定性与形状畸变，显著提升占据预测精度。

Abstract: Occupancy world models struggle to forecast moving objects due to motion uncertainty and shape distortion. A single trajectory can average plausible futures into an implausible path, and repeated re-voxelization can erode or inflate object shape even under correct motion. We introduce ShoWorld to address these challenges through separate motion and shape representations. The motion forecaster predicts probabilistic trajectories conditioned on scene layout, nearby agents and the future ego trajectory. Each hypothesis is rendered into occupancy and fused according to its probability to retain alternative futures. The shape component predicts sub-voxel points with occupancy probabilities and offsets from local geometry. These points move rigidly with the object, and voxel occupancy follows the probability that at least one occupied point falls within it. This representation reduces shape distortion under motion. ShoWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on Occ3D-nuScenes. With ground-truth occupancy and ego trajectory, it improves mIoU and IoU by 6.56 and 9.16 points overall and by 6.06 and 5.53 points on dynamic classes.

</details>


### [149] [Anchoring with Visual Evidence: Explicit Semantic Anchors for Joint Change Detection and Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24462)
*Kai Zheng,Hang-Cheng Dong,Puyi Fang,Junjia Pan,Zhenkai Wu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出显式语义锚点（ESA）作为监督式接口，将实体存在、时序外观变化和空间范围三类因子暴露给生成模块，以条件化变化描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合变化检测与描述的核心难点在于把稠密视觉证据转化为引导描述生成的语义条件。

Method: ESA 接口含三部分：预测引导读出融合视觉特征与预测变化概率；三个因子头估计锚点激活；激活经两条路径引导生成——上下文路径将三因子激活映射为三个独立记忆 token 追加到稠密视觉记忆，词表路径在每步解码产生加性词表偏置。锚点目标由现有图像与变化标注导出，无需额外锚点级语言标注；推理时全部条件来自输入图像对与模型自身预测。

Result: 在 LEVIR-MCI 与 WHU-CDC 上取得较强的联合检测与描述性能。

Conclusion: 将监督视觉属性作为显式条件用于变化描述生成是有效的。

Abstract: Joint change detection and captioning seeks to localize changes and describe them from bi-temporal remote sensing images. A key challenge is translating dense visual evidence into the semantic conditions that guide description generation. We propose Explicit Semantic Anchors (ESA), a supervised interface that exposes entity presence, temporal appearance variation, and spatial extent. A prediction-guided readout combines visual features and predicted change probabilities, and three factor heads estimate the corresponding anchor activations. These activations guide generation through two complementary routes. The context route maps the three factor activations to three separate memory tokens, one per factor, and appends them to the dense visual memory, while the lexicon route produces an additive vocabulary bias at each decoding step. Dense visual memory remains available for spatial detail and information beyond the explicit factors. Anchor targets are derived from existing images and change annotations, requiring no additional anchor-level language annotation. At inference, all conditions are computed from the input image pair and the model's own predictions. Experiments on LEVIR-MCI and WHU-CDC show strong joint detection and captioning performance, supporting the use of supervised visual attributes as explicit conditions for change description generation.

</details>


### [150] [Every Query Routes Itself: Accelerating Video Diffusion Transformers with Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24479)
*Zhen Huang,Shuwei Li,Ruizhe Yao,Xinrui Chen,Danyi Liu,Haoyuan Liang,Zhiyu Ye,Qiuyan Sun,Bingwei Chen,Yiyuan Li,Xu Zhou,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练稀疏注意力方法PQRS，仅对key聚类、让每个query独立选择key簇，并用 centroid 补偿未选簇，在Wan与HunyuanVideo四个模型上实现最快且最接近稠密输出的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer的推理成本主要由长token序列上的自注意力主导；现有聚类式稀疏注意力让同一query簇内所有query共享同一份key簇选择，作者指出这种共享限制了精度：在key簇数与密度固定时，query聚类越细注意力越准，极端情况下每个query独立选择最优。

Method: 免训练的Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention（PQRS）：只对key做聚类，每个query独立选择自己的key簇，未选中的簇用其centroid做补偿；因每个query的选择不同、不存在共享key列表的query tile，故改为遍历key簇、将选择完全相同的query分桶为稠密任务，并在一次遍历中合并各任务的部分结果，同时沿用上一去噪步的固定softmax偏移以保证数值一致性。

Result: 在Wan和HunyuanVideo家族的四个文生视频/图生视频模型上，PQRS在所评估的稀疏注意力方法中速度最快且最接近稠密输出：Wan2.1-T2V上PSNR达28.9 dB，HunyuanVideo上达35.4 dB，端到端加速1.66–2.10倍。

Conclusion: 实验表明query端细粒度路由（直至逐query选择）优于query聚类共享方案，PQRS在保持高保真度的同时取得最优加速，验证了"仅聚类key、逐query选簇"这一设计的有效性。

Abstract: Self-attention over long token sequences dominates the inference cost of video diffusion transformers. Clustering-based sparse attention clusters both queries and keys and lets all queries in a cluster share one selection of key clusters. We show that this sharing limits accuracy: with key clusters and density fixed, attention grows more accurate as the query clusters are refined, and is most accurate when each query selects on its own, which needs no query clustering at all. We therefore propose Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention (PQRS), a training-free method that clusters only keys, lets each query select its own key clusters, and compensates for the unselected ones with their centroids. Per-query selections leave no query tile with a shared key list, so PQRS traverses key clusters instead of queries, buckets queries with identical selections into dense tasks, and merges their partial results in one pass under a fixed softmax offset carried from the previous denoising step. On four text-to-video and image-to-video models from the Wan and HunyuanVideo families, PQRS is the fastest of all the sparse attention methods we evaluate and the closest to the dense output, reaching $28.9$ dB PSNR on Wan2.1-T2V and $35.4$ dB on HunyuanVideo at $1.66$--$2.10\times$ end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [151] [Comparing Each Generation with Independent Siblings Gives Video World Model Post-Training a Proper Objective for Outcome Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24512)
*Xin Li,Yiwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SibPO（Sibling-paired Policy Optimization），用同历史下独立噪声生成的“兄弟”样本来估计截断逆频率权重，使视频世界模型的训练目标成为在结果单元上严格proper的散度，从而在保持单视频保真度的同时恢复结果多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型被用作模拟器，当首帧对应多个物理结果时，重复生成应按数据中的频率产生各结果。但现有physics post-training以单一录制未来为评分目标，其期望分数对模型分布是线性的，会被"总输出最可能结果"所最大化，导致分布坍缩、多样性缺失。

Method: 从最大化录制结果的log-likelihood出发，其梯度按"模型产生该生成结果的频率的倒数"对与记录的一致性加权；该频率可由同一历史下独立噪声生成的兄弟样本估计。为此提出SibPO：兄弟样本估计截断逆频率权重用于与记录的一致性项，第二项减去同权重乘以与兄弟样本的一致性，使任意组大小下期望目标均为结果单元上的严格proper散度。

Result: 在PisaBench上，相对其奖励族外最强方法，SibPO在四个视频模型中的三个上将模拟着陆时间的KS距离降低9.1–10.2%，且不损害单视频质量；同时将估计的机器人策略成功率误差降低13%。

Conclusion: SibPO通过组内兄弟样本的逆频率加权纠正了逐视频评分目标的线性偏置，在有限组规模下仍保持严格proper性，兼顾单样本保真与多结果分布的校准，对下游机器人策略评估有实际收益。

Abstract: Video world models are used as simulators, so when a first frame leaves several physical outcomes open, repeated generations should produce each outcome as often as it occurs in the data. Physics post-training scores every generation against the single recorded future of its history, which makes single videos more faithful, but the expected score is linear in the model distribution and is maximized by always producing the most likely outcome. The log-likelihood of the recorded outcome is maximized by the data distribution, and its gradient weights agreement with the record by the inverse of how often the model produces the generated outcome, a frequency that other generations of the same history with independent noise, its siblings, can estimate. To train with this weight from a finite group, we propose SibPO, which performs Sibling-paired Policy Optimization: siblings estimate a truncated inverse-frequency weight for agreement with the record, and a second term subtracts the same weight times the agreement with the siblings, which makes the expected objective a strictly proper divergence over outcome cells at every group size. On PisaBench, SibPO lowers the KS distance to simulated landing times by 9.1-10.2% relative to the strongest method outside its reward family on three of four video models without degrading single videos, and it lowers the error of estimated robot policy success rates by 13%.

</details>


### [152] [RAID: Representation Autoencoder for Intrinsic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24521)
*Claudia Cuttano,Tamy Boubekeur,Elena Garces,Giuseppe Vecchio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAID：在DINOv3表征上解耦"重建"与"分解"，用165M参数、单次前向即可完成单图固有分解，速度比扩散方法快约100倍，质量相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图固有分解本质不适定，需从单一RGB中分离强耦合的外观因素。现有方法依赖扩散先验，带来模型庞大、需迭代采样、结果随机等问题。作者主张：精确分解并不需要大型生成模型。

Method: 两阶段设计，核心是把重建与分解解耦于强预训练表征之上：(1) X→X教师：利用DINOv3特征，通过共享解码器从各固有图重建对应固有图，无需成对的RGB-固有监督；(2) DINOv3学生：通过轻量、模态特定的投影，从RGB预测教师表征，从而可在部分标注数据集上训练，并在单次前向中预测全部固有量。最终在结构化潜空间中预测固有属性，得到165M参数的紧凑模型。

Result: 模型仅165M参数，比基于扩散的方法快约100倍；分解质量达到有竞争力或更优的水平，且对输入分辨率的鲁棒性更好；学到的投影形成可解释的、模态特定的特征空间。

Conclusion: 高保真固有分解无需大生成模型；依托强预训练表征并以"先重建、后分解"的解耦范式，可在紧凑、单次前向的框架下实现高质量、高鲁棒性与高可解释性的分解，为扩散先验路线提供了高效替代。

Abstract: Single-image intrinsic decomposition is an inherently ill-posed problem, requiring the disentanglement of strongly coupled appearance factors from a single RGB observation. Recent methods address this ambiguity with diffusion priors, at the cost of large, iterative, and often stochastic models. We show that accurate decomposition does not require a large generative model. Our key idea is to decouple reconstruction from decomposition on top of a strong pretrained representation. First, an $X \rightarrow X$ teacher learns to reconstruct each intrinsic map from DINOv3 features using a shared decoder, without paired RGB-to-intrinsic supervision. Then, a DINOv3-based student predicts the corresponding teacher representations from RGB through lightweight, modality-specific projections, enabling training on partially annotated datasets and predicting all intrinsics in a single forward pass. \textbf{RAID}, predicts intrinsic properties in the structured latent space, enabling a compact 165M-parameter model, 100x faster than diffusion-based methods. Despite its compact size, it achieves competitive or superior decomposition quality, with improved robustness to input resolution. Additionally, our learned projections yield interpretable, modality-specific feature spaces.

</details>


### [153] [SCRiD: Spatial and Contextual References for Generalizable AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24524)
*Hao Tan,Zekai Zuo,Boyang Chen,Jinghui Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCRiD框架，通过空间与上下文参考互补解释视频变化，实现可泛化的AI生成视频检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然运动与内容变化会与合成伪影相似，导致单一视频差异难以可靠判定真实性，AI生成视频检测的泛化性仍具挑战。

Method: 提出Matched-Coordinate Residual Response（MCR），在匹配亚像素采样条件下以运动与坐标参考刻画时序残差关联；提出Real-Conditioned Score Adaptation（RCSA），从真实视频学习内容条件去噪参考并探测多噪声尺度与质量视图下的响应以引导特征适配；设计Reversible Evidence Exchange（REE），通过可逆辛更新实现互补证据的信息交换。

Result: 仅用4个生成器的合成视频训练，在6个基准的88个生成器或任务子集上评测，平均ACC 72.72%、AP 74.23%，分别超越最强基线3.24和0.81个百分点；消融与鲁棒性实验验证了各组件有效性及常见后处理下的稳定性。

Conclusion: SCRiD以空间与上下文参考互补建模显著提升跨生成器与跨任务的泛化检测性能，并对后处理扰动具有稳定性。

Abstract: Generalizable AI-generated video detection remains challenging because natural motion and content variations can resemble synthesis artifacts, making isolated video discrepancies unreliable indicators of authenticity. To address this challenge, we propose Spatial and Contextual Reference-informed Detection (SCRiD), a framework that interprets video variations through complementary spatial and contextual references. Specifically, we introduce Matched-Coordinate Residual Response (MCR) to characterize temporal residual associations under motion and coordinate references with matched subpixel sampling conditions. We further develop Real-Conditioned Score Adaptation (RCSA), which learns a content-conditioned denoising reference from authentic videos and probes its responses across multiple noise scales and quality views to guide feature adaptation. To integrate these complementary sources of evidence, we design Reversible Evidence Exchange (REE) to enable information exchange through reversible symplectic updates. We train SCRiD using synthetic videos from only 4 generators and evaluate it across 88 generator or task subsets spanning 6 benchmarks. Our method achieves average ACC and AP of 72.72% and 74.23%, respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.24 and 0.81 percentage points.Ablation and robustness experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed components and the stability of SCRiD under common post-processing operations.

</details>


### [154] [Fuse Before Reconstruct: A Novel Reconstruction Paradigm for Multimodal Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24541)
*Zhi Li,Hanyu Wang,Qiyu Chen,Fei Shen,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出先融合后重建(FBR)的多模态工业异常检测新范式，在MVTec 3D-AD与Eyecandies上分别达98.3%与92.6% I-AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态联合分析的特征重建范式存在局限：模态内重建忽略跨模态互补信息，跨模态重建易受投影误差与一对多映射歧义（如同一纹理对应多种几何）影响，导致检测失败。

Method: 先融合多模态特征再重建：(1) 双向跨模态注意力实现图像与点云特征的深度互补，通过通道对齐与动态空间先验集成缓解投影偏差；(2) 可学习的逐像素门控融合策略，在优化局部特征组合的同时保持多模态结构一致性；(3) 双路径协同重建，从融合表示中恢复各原始模态特征，并引入跨模态一致性约束消除映射歧义。

Result: MVTec 3D-AD上I-AUROC 98.3%，Eyecandies上92.6%，分别超越先前SOTA +2.9%与+4.1%；实验验证有效。

Conclusion: FBR通过"先融合后重建"规避投影误差与一对多映射歧义，验证了融合表征加一致性约束的重建路线在多模态工业异常检测中的优越性。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection plays a pivotal role in precision manufacturing, where joint analysis of multimodal data emerges as a critical approach to enhance detection accuracy. Feature reconstruction, a dominant paradigm for such joint analysis, is primarily implemented through intra-modal and cross-modal methods, both of which are plagued by significant limitations. Intra-modal reconstruction neglects complementary cross-modal information, while its cross-modal counterpart is prone to projection errors and ambiguous one-to-many mappings (e.g., same textures mapping to multiple geometries), leading to detection failures. To address these limitations, we propose a novel feature reconstruction paradigm, Fuse Before Reconstruct (FBR), which first fuses multimodal features and then performs reconstruction for anomaly detection. Firstly, we propose a bidirectional cross-modal attention mechanism to achieve deep feature complementarity between image and point cloud features, mitigating projection bias through channel alignment and dynamic spatial prior integration. Besides, a learnable pixel-wise gated fusion strategy is adopted to preserve multi-modal structural consistency while optimizing local feature combination. Finally, dual-path collaborative reconstruction is implemented to recover original modal features from the fused representations, where cross-modal consistency constraints effectively eliminate mapping ambiguities. Extensive experiments show that FBR achieves 98.3% I-AUROC on MVTec 3D-AD and 92.6% I-AUROC on Eyecandies, surpassing previous state-of-the-art methods by +2.9% and +4.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [155] [SCR: Detecting Backdoor-Poisoned Samples through Clean-Reference Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24558)
*Yi Yang,Jinyang Huang,Feng-Qi Cui,Yu-Tong Guo,Sirui Zhao,Haiming Jin,Weihua Li,Quan Bai,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义反事实残差化（SCR）方法，利用少量干净参考样本进行样本特异的特征重构以建模良性语义变化，从而检测后门投毒样本，在12种攻击、3个数据集上平均TPR超99.5%、FPR低于2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络易受后门攻击，攻击者通过向训练数据注入投毒样本操控模型预测。检测难点在于：合法的语义变化会在学到的表示中掩盖投毒相关信号；现有检测器依赖类级统计量，容易把良性特征偏差误报，同时漏掉混入类分布的投毒样本。

Method: 语义反事实残差化（SCR）：使用一小批干净参考样本，通过样本特异的特征重构来刻画良性语义变化；随后通过检测样本相对这些干净基线的一致性偏差来识别投毒样本，且无需关于攻击的先验知识。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet上覆盖12种dirty-label与clean-label攻击进行评测；在少量干净参考集下，各数据集攻击平均TPR均超99.5%，FPR低于2%，在macro-TPR上优于8个代表性检测基线。

Conclusion: SCR通过以干净参考基线对良性语义变化进行残差化建模，有效克服类级统计检测的误报与漏报问题，实现高检出率、低误报率且与攻击类型无关的通用后门样本检测。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, in which adversaries manipulate model predictions by injecting poisoned samples into training data. Detecting these samples remains challenging because legitimate semantic variation can obscure poisoning-related signals in learned representations. Consequently, class-level statistics used by existing detectors may flag benign feature deviations while overlooking poisoned samples that blend into the class distribution. In this paper, we propose Semantic Counterfactual Residualization (SCR), which uses a small set of clean reference samples to account for benign variation through sample-specific feature reconstruction. SCR identifies poisoned samples by detecting coherent deviations from these clean baselines, without requiring prior knowledge of the attacks. We evaluate SCR through experiments covering twelve dirty-label and clean-label attacks across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet. With a small clean-reference set, SCR achieves attack-averaged true positive rates above 99.5\% and false positive rates below 2\% on each dataset, outperforming eight representative detection baselines in macro-TPR.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Appearance: Evidence-Guided Programmatic 3D Scene Reconstruction with Agentic Lighting Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24565)
*Zeting Luo,Yifan Li,Shuai Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LightHarness——基于VLM的智能体框架，将显式光照推理引入迭代式图像到代码的3D场景重建，无需额外训练或可微渲染即可提升阴影与复杂光照下的重建精度并支持重打光等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到代码的3D场景重建方法主要依赖外观相似度，难以区分几何、材质与光照三类误差来源；而光照通过与几何、材质的交互（明暗、投影、反射）塑造场景外观，是关键但未被显式建模的线索。

Method: 1）利用几何、本征（intrinsic）与光照三类互补线索联合优化场景结构与外观；2）提出Lighting Reasoning Memory，维护光源假设及其证据与修订历史，贯穿迭代过程；3）支持对部分可见及不可见光源的推理，由证据引导的验证驱动后续修订；4）全程无需额外训练或可微渲染。

Result: 大量实验表明，在具有挑战性的阴影与复杂光照条件下，图像到代码的重建精度优于现有方法，并进一步支撑场景编辑、物体插入与多样化重打光等下游应用。

Conclusion: 将显式光照推理嵌入智能体重建循环，可有效解耦几何、材质与光照误差，为可编辑3D内容的生成与视觉仿真提供更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Image-to-code methods for 3D scene reconstruction produce executable code that generates editable digital content and supports visual simulation. However, existing methods primarily rely on appearance similarity, which provides limited guidance for distinguishing errors in geometry, materials, and illumination. Light shapes scene appearance through its interactions with geometry and materials, influencing shading, cast shadows, and reflections. We therefore present LightHarness, a VLM-based agentic framework that incorporates explicit lighting reasoning into iterative scene reconstruction. To be specific, we leverage complementary geometric, intrinsic, and illumination cues to jointly refine scene structure and appearance, and propose Lighting Reasoning Memory to maintain light-source hypotheses, which supports evidence, and revision history throughout iterations. Together, they support reasoning about partially visible and unseen sources, with evidence-guided verification directing subsequent revisions. Our framework reconstructs explicit scenes through coherent reasoning about geometry, materials, and illumination without additional training or differentiable rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior image-to-code reconstruction accuracy under challenging shadows and complex illumination, further supporting downstream scene editing, object insertion, and versatile relighting.

</details>


### [157] [BaseRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of a Base Vision-Language Model via Training-Free Harness Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24578)
*Mingda Zhang,Yuanhao Dong,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Yanjin Li,Zhenyu Liu,Xiao Huang,Zihao Yang,Jian Wang,Pinjia He,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BaseRSI"递归框架内化"范式与ReGap/ReKey机制，通过免训练权重编辑将harness增益写入基座模型内部，在十个视觉推理基准上稳定提升VLM表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归自我改进（RSI）存在三大局限：增益被束缚在基座模型外部的prompt与工具harness中；黑箱监督仅能读取模型输出；编辑的写入位置与强度预先固定、不够精确。因此希望把harness带来的改进真正内化到基座模型权重中。

Method: 提出BaseRSI范式：基座模型先改进自身harness，再通过免训练编辑将harness的增益逐轮写入自身权重。配套ReGap机制：以harness在单个transformer block处产生的高维激活差作为该block的监督信号，并在低维子空间内沿该信号更新权重。进一步提出ReKey算法：把该信号回归写入每个block的少量key-value神经元，并在模型自身响应上度量每次编辑的位置与强度。所有角色均由前向传播完成，不同位置的编辑可在同一checkpoint中组合而无需重训。

Result: 在十个视觉推理基准上，BaseRSI对基座视觉语言模型在分布内与留出基准上均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 将harness增益内化进权重是可行且有效的方向，免训练、可组合的编辑机制能实现递归自我改进落于基座模型本身。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) is moving from the harness of prompts and tools around a base model toward the model itself. However, existing RSI still faces key challenges, including harness-bound gains that stay outside the base model, black-box supervision that reads only the model's outputs, and imprecise edits whose site and strength are fixed in advance. To address these challenges, we propose BaseRSI, a new paradigm of Recursive Harness Internalization: the base model improves its harness, then writes what the harness adds into its own weights through training-free edits, round after round on its own responses. To support this paradigm, we introduce ReGap, a mechanism that supervises every transformer block with the high-dimensional activation difference that the harness makes at that block alone, and updates the block's weights along this signal in a low-dimensional subspace. Building on this mechanism, we further propose ReKey, an algorithm that writes this signal by regression into a few key-value neurons of every block, and measures the site and strength of each edit on the model's own responses. Moreover, the base model fills every role of this recursion with forward passes alone, and its edits, written at different sites, compose in one checkpoint without refitting. Experimental results on ten visual reasoning benchmarks show that BaseRSI consistently improves a base vision-language model both in distribution and on held-out benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/BaseRSI.

</details>


### [158] [ALIVE: Bring Foundation Features to Life with Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24579)
*Rong Zou,Haiqing Hao,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALIVE：一种基于事件相机的方法，利用后续事件流增量更新冻结DINOv3编码器的多层特征，仅用合成数据训练，在真实事件数据上无需适配即可用于检测、分割和深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型特征在图像观测间会因视觉内容变化而"过期"，刷新需要新帧并反复运行昂贵的图像编码器，计算开销大。

Method: 以锚点图像为起点，用后续事件流对冻结DINOv3编码器提取的多层原生特征进行传输与校正；轻量更新器端到端训练，仅使用合成数据，无下游任务监督，直接迁移到真实事件流。

Result: 在3个真实数据集、5个任务-数据集设置（目标检测、语义分割、深度估计）上，全部指标优于所有事件基线。DSEC上锚帧200ms时，检测提升30.9 mAP、分割提升11.6 mIoU、深度AbsRel改善15.9%；相比图像重编码，FLOPs减少82.4%，延迟降低2.5倍。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的增量特征刷新可高效维持基础模型特征的时效性，且合成数据训练具备良好的真实世界泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual foundation models provide powerful representations for diverse tasks, but their features become stale as visual content changes between image observations. Refreshing them requires new frames and repeated runs of a computationally expensive image encoder. We introduce ALIVE, an event-based method that incrementally updates native foundation model features between image observations. Given an anchor image, ALIVE uses subsequent events to transport and correct existing multi-layer features extracted by a frozen DINOv3 encoder. Its lightweight updater is trained end-to-end using only synthetic data, without downstream task supervision, and transfers directly to real-world event streams without any adaptation. Across three real-world datasets and five task-dataset settings spanning object detection, semantic segmentation, and depth estimation, ALIVE outperforms all evaluated event-based methods in every metric. On DSEC, when the anchor frame is 200,ms old, ALIVE improves over anchor-image predictions by 30.9,mAP in detection, 11.6,mIoU in segmentation, and 15.9% in depth AbsRel. Compared with image re-encoding, ALIVE reduces FLOPs by 82.4% and latency by a factor of 2.5.

</details>


### [159] [DAL: Dynamic Angular Loss for Imbalanced Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24597)
*Salman Mohammad,Furkan Kasım,Manuel Günther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于负角度统计的数据驱动角度边界估计与批次自适应角度间隔（BAAM），结合动态加权交叉熵（DWCE）构成动态角度损失（DAL），用于类别严重不平衡的医学图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类中少数但临床重要类常被多数类压制。现有角度型softmax（ABS）损失虽判别性强，但其在"类别数少且严重不平衡"场景下的行为理解不足；以往估计ABS内在参数的方法依赖"类别数多"的假设，在医学场景不成立。

Method: 利用负角度统计量推导尺度参数的数据驱动上下界及角度间隔的上界，据此构建批次自适应角度间隔BAAM，并与动态加权交叉熵DWCE组合成动态角度损失DAL。

Result: 在皮肤科、眼科、胃肠内镜四个高度不平衡医学图像分类基准上，方法持续获得更高的平衡准确率，并使各类别召回率变异系数最低；多数类性能略有下降，但少数类增益显著。

Conclusion: DAL可在严重类别不平衡下提供更可靠、公平的分类器，兼顾整体平衡性与各类别间召回一致性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Class imbalance remains a major obstacle in medical image classification, where rare but clinically important classes are often overshadowed by majority categories. Although current strategies for reducing bias, including angle-based softmax (ABS) losses, offer strong discriminative features, their behavior in settings with a small number of severely imbalanced classes remains insufficiently understood. Previous approaches that estimate the intrinsic parameters of ABS rely on the assumption of many classes, which does not hold in medical settings. We derive data-driven upper and lower bounds for the scale parameter and an upper bound for the angular margin using negative-angle statistics, and use these to drive a batch-adaptive angular margin (BAAM), which we combine with dynamically-weighted cross entropy (DWCE) to define our dynamic angular loss (DAL). Across four highly imbalanced medical image classification benchmarks spanning dermatology, ophthalmology, and gastrointestinal endoscopy, our method consistently achieves superior balanced accuracy and the lowest coefficient of variation of per-class recall. Although this entails a small performance reduction on majority classes, the gains on minority categories are substantial, yielding a reliable and equitable overall classifier. Our source code is available at: https://github.com/msalman95/DAL.

</details>


### [160] [EgoJev: Finding Evidence in Egocentric Video by Reading VLM Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24616)
*Chunggi Lee,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练帧选择器 EgoJev：利用冻结 VLM 从带标签缩略图的输出分布中读取证据时刻，再在定位窗口内重采样，用少量帧显著提升长自我中心视频问答精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时自我中心视频的问题常依赖极短暂的瞬间（如手放下物体的那一刻），均匀采样稀疏帧的 VLM 易错过证据；现有免训练方法依赖独立图文模型打分或让 VLM 直接写出时间戳，定位不可靠且需额外模型。

Method: 对照实验发现：在相同候选帧下，VLM 从带标签帧中"做选择"（从输出分布读出选择）比图文打分或写出时间跨度更常命中证据，且能在一次决策中比较全部候选。据此构建 EgoJev：仅用单个冻结 VLM，先由带标签缩略图读出证据所在位置，再对指向的窗口二次观察，并按所得分布的量化分位放置少量答案帧；设备可在录制时预编码粗粒度缩略图。

Result: Qwen3.5-4B、8 帧设定下，QAEgo4D 准确率由均匀采样的 51.2% 提升至 62.0%；在 QAEgo4D 与 MyEgo 上优于近期免训练帧选择器；单层版本可在 Mac 与 iPhone 上运行。

Conclusion: 以"让 VLM 在带标签候选中做选择"替代外部检索器是更可靠的免训练时刻定位范式；单一冻结模型即可完成选择、回答与证据时间回传，且具备端侧部署可行性。

Abstract: Questions about long egocentric video often depend on one brief moment, such as the second a hand puts an object down. A vision-language model (VLM) that sees a few evenly spaced frames usually misses it. Training-free methods rank frames with a separate image-text model or ask the VLM to write a time. In a controlled test, the VLM itself locates the moment better when it chooses among labeled frames, and we read this choice from its output distribution. With the same candidates, this choice lands on the evidence more often than image-text scores or a written time span, and it compares all candidates in one decision. Based on this finding, we propose EgoJev, a training-free frame selector for wearable assistants. EgoJev reads where the evidence is from labeled thumbnails and looks again at the windows it points to. It places its few answer frames at the quantiles of the resulting distribution. One frozen VLM selects, answers and returns the evidence time, with no second model. A wearable device can encode its coarse thumbnails while it records. With Qwen3.5-4B and 8 answer frames, EgoJev raises QAEgo4D accuracy from 51.2% with uniform sampling to 62.0%, and it outperforms recent training-free frame selectors on QAEgo4D and MyEgo. A single-level version runs on a Mac and an iPhone.

</details>


### [161] [H3Remover: Reinforcing Video Object Removal without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24633)
*Kangjie Chen,Xiangyu Li,Jinhao Deng,Dongbin Zhang,Chaoda Zheng,Shijia Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Choo Sin Wai,Boyi Sun,Puyi Wang,Yu Zhang,Xianming Liu,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 H3Remover，在无成对"无目标"视频训练数据条件下，通过强化学习式多奖励优化（目标移除深度奖励、背景几何奖励、外观保护奖励 + 冻结 HPSv3）微调 MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA 的 LoRA，实现更彻底的目标移除与更稳定的场景保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频编辑器虽具备丰富的移除与补全先验，但仍存在两类问题：一是目标未被彻底移除（如仅重着色等表面编辑），二是误改与目标无关的背景内容。且缺少成对的无目标训练视频，难以直接监督。

Method: 以 MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA 为骨干并冻结，用 DiffusionART 仅优化 LoRA 适配器。设计多奖励约束：(1) 目标移除奖励——偏好与"显露更远内容"一致的标定深度变化，抑制重着色等浅层编辑；(2) 背景几何奖励——保持相对深度排序，限制意外场景变化；(3) 外观奖励——仅在源图派生的受保护区域内约束 RGB 变化，同时豁免目标区域及估计的阴影/反射效应；(4) 冻结 HPSv3 提供合成内容的感知质量反馈。评估区域在各候选间保持固定。

Result: 相较原始 H3 取得显著的重建提升；定性对比显示目标移除更有效，且周边场景内容保持更好。

Conclusion: 无需成对无目标数据，仅凭精心设计的多维奖励与 LoRA 微调即可显著增强视频目标移除性能，兼顾移除彻底性与背景保真度。

Abstract: Pretrained video editors possess rich removal and completion priors, yet may retain targets or alter unrelated content. We present H3Remover, which reinforces MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA for video object removal without paired object-free training targets. To discourage superficial edits such as recoloring, a target-removal reward favors calibrated depth changes consistent with revealing farther content. To limit unintended scene changes, a background-geometry reward encourages preservation of relative depth ordering. To preserve background appearance without retaining unwanted shadows or reflections, an appearance reward constrains RGB changes in source-derived protected regions while exempting targets and estimated effects. Frozen HPSv3 complements these constraints with perceptual-quality feedback for synthesized content. Evaluation regions remain fixed across candidates, and DiffusionART optimizes LoRA adapters with the backbone frozen. Experiments show substantial reconstruction gains over original H3, with qualitative comparisons demonstrating more effective object removal and better preservation of surrounding scene content.

</details>


### [162] [AggCLIP: Aggregating CLIP for Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24643)
*Zhi Li,Hanyu Wang,Fei Shen,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AggCLIP 框架，通过几何感知与上下文感知的空间聚合模块以及双流提示对比损失，改进冻结 CLIP 在零样本多模态异常检测中的细粒度缺陷定位与跨模态语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本多模态异常检测需在无目标类别训练数据下同时检测外观与几何缺陷。现有基于 CLIP 的方法存在三大问题：(i) 点云 3D 渲染到 2D 时几何信息严重丢失；(ii) 原始 CLIP patch 嵌入产生的 2D 特征图空间噪声大，且模态融合层次浅、偏后；(iii) 解耦的处理流导致跨模态语义严重失配。

Method: AggCLIP 显式引入局部几何结构与视觉空间上下文以适配冻结 CLIP：1) 几何感知空间聚合（GSA）模块，通过密度自适应邻域加权对反投影的 3D 点特征进行细化；2) 上下文感知空间聚合（CSA）模块，借助轻量可训练适配器平滑多尺度 2D patch 嵌入；3) 双流提示对比（DSPC）损失，在点云流与 RGB 流之间对齐"正常/异常"的抽象概念，抑制语义漂移。

Result: 在 MVTec 3D-AD 与 Eyecandies 基准上，于 one-vs-rest 与跨数据集零样本设置下均取得 SOTA，像素级 AUPRO 最高超过最强竞品 2.9%。

Conclusion: 显式建模局部几何结构与空间上下文、并在跨模态层面施加语义对齐约束，可有效缓解渲染信息损失、特征噪声与模态失配问题，验证了该思路在零样本多模态异常检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection (ZSMAD) aims to identify both appearance and geometric defects in novel categories without relying on target-specific training data, making it especially valuable in real-world scenarios constrained by data scarcity, privacy concerns, or high annotation costs. While recent approaches successfully adapt vision-language models like CLIP to achieve this open-vocabulary generalization, they still face significant challenges: (i) severe geometric information loss during 3D-to-2D point cloud rendering; (ii) spatially noisy 2D feature maps produced by raw CLIP patch embeddings, compounded by shallow late-stage modality fusion; and (iii) severe cross-modal semantic misalignment due to decoupled processing streams. To address these limitations, we propose AggCLIP, a novel framework that adapts frozen CLIP representations for ZSMAD by explicitly incorporating local geometric structure and visual spatial context. Specifically, to accurately localize fine-grained defects, we design a Geometry-Aware Spatial Aggregation (GSA) module that refines back-projected 3D point features via density-adaptive neighborhood weighting, alongside a Context-Aware Spatial Aggregation (CSA) module that smooths multi-scale 2D patch embeddings using a lightweight trainable adapter. Furthermore, to prevent semantic drift between modalities, we introduce a Dual-Stream Prompt Contrastive (DSPC) loss, which aligns the abstract concepts of normality and abnormality across the point cloud and RGB streams. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed AggCLIP framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies benchmarks under both one-vs-rest and cross-dataset zero-shot settings, notably surpassing the strongest competitive methods by up to 2.9% in pixel-level AUPRO.

</details>


### [163] [Predicting Species Splits: A Challenging Fine-Grained Benchmark for Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24672)
*Christian Lange,Joakim Bruslund Haurum,Bingchen Zhao,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PS-SPLIT大规模细粒度类别发现基准，基于真实分类学拆分构建，并引入"按类别拆分进行发现"的新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别发现基准趋于饱和，难以反映真实世界的发现场景，不利于对新方法进行长期评估；同时真实发现往往来自对已知类别的细分，现有基准缺乏对此的刻画。

Method: 借鉴分类学拆分（原被视作单一物种被细分为多个独立物种）过程，构建PS-SPLIT：来自257个近期经专家验证的分类学拆分、771个物种、79K张全球分布的高质量社区采集鸟类图像，并附带地理元数据与完整分类层级；据此定义"按类别拆分进行发现"任务，即判定哪些已知类别应被细分为更细的子类，并将四种广义类别发现方法适配至该设定进行评估。

Result: PS-SPLIT中由真实拆分产生的类间视觉差异极为细微，常需专业人工具辨识，构成高难度基准；现有方法在该任务上表现有限，留有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: PS-SPLIT为细粒度类别发现提供了具挑战性且贴近现实的基准，有望推动该方向研究，并揭示基于类别拆分的发现任务仍有广阔研究空间。

Abstract: Widely used category discovery benchmarks have become increasingly saturated and often fail to reflect real-world discovery settings, making long-term evaluation of new methods difficult. We introduce PS-SPLIT, a large-scale fine-grained benchmark for category discovery, motivated by taxonomic splitting: the real-world process by which what was once considered a single species is found to comprise multiple distinct ones. PS-SPLIT contains 79K globally distributed, high-quality, community-collected bird images spanning 771 species, derived from 257 recent real expert-verified taxonomic splits, with associated geographic metadata and full taxonomic hierarchy. The visual differences between categories arising from real taxonomic splits can be very subtle, often requiring significant human expertise to tell apart, making PS-SPLIT a challenging benchmark which we hope will assist in advancing fine-grained category discovery research. Based on taxonomic splitting, we introduce the task of discovery by category splitting, where the goal is to determine which known categories should be subdivided into new finer-grained subcategories. We adapt four generalized category discovery methods to this setting, evaluate existing methods, and show that there is substantial room for future progress.

</details>


### [164] [DrawScore: Evaluating Generated CAD Models against Engineering Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24681)
*Haorui Chen,Shiteng Lu,Zhanyu Guo,Zaixi Li,Yunhe Ni,Yaosong Du,Yichen Xiang,Xiande Huang,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出体积IoU、Chamfer Distance与表面F-score等CAD生成评测指标难以惩罚小范围但关键的局部错误，通过统一积分形式分析其误差衰减规律，提出基于三视图投影的DrawScore指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD生成指标（体积IoU、Chamfer Distance、表面F-score）几乎不惩罚仅占部件体积或表面积很小比例的局部错误（如孔位偏移、凹槽开在错误面），而这类错误在制造中却是致命的，因此需要能够有效暴露局部错误的新评测指标。

Method: 将现有指标统一写成积分形式，并证明局部错误的权重随其尺寸按$(k+q)$次幂衰减（$k$为积分域维数，$q$由惩罚函数决定）；据此提出DrawScore：将两个模型投影为三幅带可见线与隐藏线的正交工程视图，沿投影曲线施加饱和惩罚，使局部错误权重仅线性衰减。

Result: 受控实验验证了理论预测的衰减阶数；在现有CAD生成智能体的输出上，DrawScore能够揭示被IoU评为近乎完美的输出中的错误；作为强化学习奖励可提升生成质量；在工程图到CAD生成任务中，可作为无需3D真值、无需额外奖励模型的测试时验证器。

Conclusion: DrawScore通过降维投影与饱和惩罚将局部错误的权重衰减从高次幂降至线性，显著提升了对制造关键局部错误的敏感度，并兼具评测指标、强化学习奖励与测试时验证器三重作用。

Abstract: Automatic CAD generation is commonly evaluated with volumetric IoU, Chamfer Distance, and surface F-score, which barely penalize local errors that occupy only a small fraction of a part's volume or surface area, such as a misplaced hole or a slot on the wrong face, even though such errors are critical to manufacturing. We formalize these metrics in a unified integral form and show that the weight of a local error decays as the $(k+q)$-th power of its size, where $k$ is the dimension of the integration domain and $q$ is determined by the penalty function. Guided by this analysis, we propose DrawScore, which projects both models into three orthographic views with visible and hidden lines, as in engineering drawings, and applies a saturating penalty along the projected curves, so that the weight of a local error decays only linearly. Controlled experiments confirm the predicted decay orders, and on outputs of existing CAD generation agents, DrawScore exposes errors in outputs that IoU rates as nearly perfect. DrawScore further improves generation as a reinforcement learning reward and, for CAD generation from engineering drawings, serves as a test-time verifier without 3D ground truth or an additional reward model. Our code is available at https://github.com/UESTC-Simon/DrawScore.

</details>


### [165] [Improving Zero-shot 3D Semantic Mapping in Offroad with Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24696)
*Eerik Alamikkotervo,Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以各类别球形度-高程分布作为几何锚点，仅用视觉基础模型高置信点拟合该先验，再与视觉预测融合生成语义3D地图，在RELLIS-3D与WildScenes上相对SAM3基线提升mIoU与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在结构化场景零样本语义预测准确，但在自然越野环境中性能下降；而3D点云在视觉模型失效时可提供判别语义的几何信息，二者互补。

Method: 为每个语义类别拟合球形度-高程分布作为几何先验（锚点）；拟合时仅采用视觉基础模型预测置信度最高的点以保证先验质量；随后将该几何先验与视觉基础模型预测融合，得到最终语义地图。

Result: RELLIS-3D上较SAM3基线mIoU提升6.3、精度提升8.9；WildScenes上mIoU提升1.7、精度提升2.1。

Conclusion: 几何先验能有效弥补视觉基础模型在越野自然场景中的语义预测不足，二者融合可提升语义3D建图精度；代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Semantic 3D mapping is a powerful representation that enables platform agnostic mobile robot navigation in challenging offroad conditions. Current vision foundation models can accurately predict semantics zero-shot on structured scenes but performance is compromised in natural environments. On the other hand, 3D point clouds encode valuable information for discriminating semantics in scenarios where vision foundation models fail. In this work, we propose fitting a sphericity-elevation distribution for each semantic class to act as geometric anchor. Only the points with the most confident vision foundation model predictions are considered when fitting to produce high quality geometric prior distributions. The geometric prior is fused with the vision foundation model prediction to produce the final semantic mapping. The proposed prior improves SAM3 baseline by +6.3 in mIoU and +8.9 in accuracy on the RELLIS-3D dataset, and by +1.7 in mIoU and +2.1 in accuracy on the WildScenes dataset. The source code will be made public upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [166] [Can Omni-modal LLMs Understand Spoken Intentions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24706)
*Zijie Xin,Tianyi Wang,Daoze Zhang,Jie Yang,Changli Tang,Qijie Wei,Fengyun Rao,Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 O-IN-Bench 基准，评估全模态大模型对"语音意图"的理解：模型观看带原生语音的完整视频，输出一段文本回复或 [SILENCE]；该基准含 1000 条中英文样本（5 大类、16 子类）、对比样本对与样本级评分量表，并由 O-IN-Engine 逐项核验，显示"识别请求"与"满足需求"是可分离的两种能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态 LLM 可直接通过语音/视频通话应答，响应依赖用户与助手共享的场景，同一句话在不同情境下可能需不同帮助或无需回应。现有视听基准多以文本单独给出问题，交互类基准又多对整段回复整体打分，因而无法判断模型是否识别出用户正在求助，以及识别后回复是否满足需求。

Method: 构建 O-IN-Bench：输入为带原生语音的完整视频，输出为单条文本回复，无需回应时输出 [SILENCE]。数据集含 1000 条中英文样本，覆盖 5 大类、16 子类；采用对比样本对（同一请求搭配不同场景条件）测试响应是否随上下文变化；采用样本级评分量表分别评判语言、意图与内容，并由 O-IN-Engine 依据视频对每条评分标准进行核验。共评测 30 种全模态模型配置。

Result: 最佳总体分为 59.6（Gemini3.7-Flash）；其意图判定通过率 74.4%，但内容判定通过率仅 42.6%。当语音是同伴间对话而非对助手的请求时，强模型仍常持续作答；当回复需同时满足多人时，最高分仅 25.9。

Conclusion: 识别请求与满足请求要求是两种相互独立的能力，O-IN-Bench 对二者分别度量；当前全模态 LLM 在意图识别上明显强于内容满足，且在拒绝应答与多方需求场景下仍存在显著短板。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (omni-modal LLMs) can respond to spoken requests directly through voice and video calls. Such requests rely on the scene shared by the user and the assistant, so the same utterance may call for different help or for no response. Most audio-visual benchmarks provide questions as separate text input, and interactive benchmarks mostly score a response as a whole. It thus remains unclear whether a model recognizes when the user is asking for help, and whether its reply meets the user's need once the request is recognized. We propose O-IN-Bench to evaluate how well omni-modal LLMs understand spoken intentions. A model watches a complete video with native speech and outputs one text response, or [SILENCE] when no response is needed. O-IN-Bench contains 1,000 Chinese and English samples in five categories and 16 subcategories. Contrastive pairs combine the same request with different scene conditions to test whether the response changes with the context. A sample-specific rubric judges language, intent, and content separately, and O-IN-Engine verifies each rubric against the video. Across 30 omni-modal model configurations, the best overall score is 59.6, achieved by Gemini3.7-Flash. Gemini3.7-Flash passes 74.4% of the intent judgments but only 42.6% of the content judgments. When the speech is a conversation between peers rather than a request to the assistant, even strong models often keep responding. When a reply must satisfy several people, the best score is only 25.9. These results indicate that recognizing a request and meeting its requirements are separate abilities, and O-IN-Bench measures each of them.

</details>


### [167] [DRIFT: Detachable Reasoning for Runtime-Flexible and Efficient End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24719)
*Emad Aghajanzadeh,Bethany Anne Wong,Leonhard Kunze,Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Nael Fasfous,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Pierpaolo Mori,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRIFT，将轻量语言模型（<150M）与蒸馏代理推理特征结合，实现可运行时开关语言推理的端到端自动驾驶框架，255M参数下在Bench2Drive达DS 97.03、SR 89.39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM端到端自动驾驶计算开销大，且难以在运行时自由开关语言推理而不损害闭环性能，阻碍实际部署。

Method: 1）将中间驾驶表示高效投影到语言嵌入空间，使紧凑LM完成场景描述与动作注释（VQA式）；2）引入运行时灵活规划模块，训练时使用从LM蒸馏的代理推理特征，推理时可条件于LM或代理特征，从而可完全绕过LM。

Result: 仅255M参数，在Bench2Drive上按选定推理率交错LM与代理推理，DS达97.03、SR 89.39%（三次运行均值），媲美最强非语言模型且远小于现有VLM方案；在NAVSIM与AlpaSim真实基准上取得有竞争力结果，计算成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 轻量语言能力与蒸馏代理推理可在保持驾驶性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，并支持运行时按部署约束灵活启用/禁用语言推理。

Abstract: The integration of vision-language models (VLMs) into end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has demonstrated strong potential by providing scene description and reasoning capabilities. However, VLMs are computationally expensive, and switching language reasoning on and off at runtime without hurting closed-loop performance has not been demonstrated, which hinders practical adoption. To address these issues, we propose DRIFT, an E2E autonomous driving framework that integrates a lightweight language model (LM) for visual question answering and action commentary. By efficiently projecting intermediate driving representations into the language embedding space, we demonstrate that a compact LM (<150M parameters) can achieve strong scene description and action commentary. Furthermore, we introduce a runtime-flexible planning module that is trained on surrogate reasoning features distilled from the LM and can be conditioned on either the LM or the surrogate reasoning features at inference. This design enables bypassing the LM entirely when required by deployment constraints, without sacrificing driving performance. With only 255M parameters, DRIFT reaches up to a Driving Score (DS) of 97.03 and a Success Rate of 89.39% (mean over three runs) on Bench2Drive by interleaving its LM and surrogate reasoning at a chosen reasoning rate. This is on par with the strongest non-language models while being far more compact than existing VLM-based models. DRIFT further achieves competitive results on the real-world NAVSIM and AlpaSim benchmarks at significantly lower computational cost than existing VLM-based approaches. Project page: https://drift-e2e.github.io/

</details>


### [168] [Multi-Band Galaxy Novel-View Synthesis with Radiative Gaussian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24723)
*Yuzhe Wu,Jialin Zhu,Doğa Yılmaz,Daisuke Kawata,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向星系多波段观测的物理启发式高斯泼溅方法 GalaxyGS，用于星系视图合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB 场景中 GS 已成功，但星系观测由海量天体的多波段信号组成，存在复杂的跨波段相互作用，且光在高度非均匀介质中传播；其物理过程给模型表示与优化带来挑战，现有研究尚未涉及。

Method: 提出物理启发的 Gaussian Splatting 方法 GalaxyGS，其组件具有物理含义，分别刻画不同天体的能量发射机制以及天体间的光传播过程。由于缺乏直接可比的基线方法，采用多种可能的替代方案进行对比。

Result: 在多个数据集与评价指标上，GalaxyGS 在准确性和物理保真度方面均优于替代方法。

Conclusion: 将 GS 从 RGB 扩展到星系多波段观测是可行且有效的，物理先验的引入有助于提升表示的准确性与物理一致性。

Abstract: We propose a novel view synthesis tool for galaxies based on Gaussian Splatting (GS). Unlike RGB data, where GS has achieved success, galaxy observations consist of multi-band signals from a massive number of bodies, contain complex patterns of cross-band interactions, and capture light propagation in highly heterogeneous media. As a result, the underlying physical process poses great challenges in model representation and optimization, which have not been investigated in existing research. To this end, we propose a physically inspired Gaussian Splatting method, GalaxyGS, which is equipped with physically meaningful components that capture both the energy emission mechanism of different astronomical bodies and the light propagation between them. In the absence of directly comparable baseline methods, we adopt a wide range of possible alternative approaches. Comparisons conclusively demonstrate that GalaxyGS is better in accuracy and physical fidelity across datasets and metrics.

</details>


### [169] [Generalization of Text Line Segmentation for HTR in Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24726)
*Gayan H. Pathirage,Stephan M. Unter,Simon Corbillé,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究Hi-SAM、YOLO、RT-DETR等目标检测模型在历史文档文本行分割中的泛化与小样本能力，并指出mAP不足以评估分割质量，提出以CRNN识别CER衡量下游效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆与档案馆难以为每个新馆藏制作大量标注样本，因此需要模型具备跨馆藏泛化能力，并支持极少样本微调。

Method: 在9个版式与语言差异显著的公开数据集上评测三种SOTA检测模型（Hi-SAM、YOLO、RT-DETR）的跨数据集泛化性能；进一步以每目标数据集少量样本进行少样本微调；并分析mAP作为文本行分割评价指标的局限性。

Result: 基于所分割文本行的CRNN识别在不同数据集上页面级CER为2.48%–7.07%，足以支撑阅读理解和简单文本检索；同时量化了不同使用场景下训练与推理的资源与时间开销。

Conclusion: 目标检测模型在历史文档文本行分割上具备可用的泛化与少样本适应能力，应以端到端识别误差而非mAP评估分割质量；资源与时间分析可为按可用资源选择模型与硬件提供依据。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate the generalization capability of state-of-the-art object detection models (Hi-SAM, YOLO, and RT-DETR) for the automatic segmentation of text lines in historical documents. Generalization is needed because it is not practical for museums and archives to produce large amounts of labeled samples for each new collection. We also evaluate the few-shot setting by fine-tuning the networks with only a small number of training samples per target dataset. To this end, we use nine different datasets that differ significantly in visual layout and language. Additionally, we discuss why mean average precision (mAP), a classic evaluation metric for object detection, is insufficient for assessing the quality of a text line segmentation model. Depending on the dataset, our CRNN models achieve character error rates (CER) ranging from 2.48% to 7.07% per page for the segmented text lines. This is sufficient for reading comprehension and simple text searches. Finally, we quantify the resource and time requirements of training and inference in different use case settings. This forms the basis for more informed decisions about model and hardware choices based on available resources.

</details>


### [170] [HandWorldBench: A Unified Benchmark for Hand-Centric World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24731)
*Zhaolu Wang,Jiacheng Xu,Lihong Wang,Shuqing Wang,Jianjie Fang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Rongze Tang,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 HandWorldBench，一个系统评估以手为中心的世界模型的统一基准，覆盖人手、仿真灵巧手与真实灵巧手，从视觉质量、手部保真度、外观一致性、指令遵循、物理一致性和时序连贯性六个维度进行评估，并基于 600+ 小时素材构建 20,500 段高质量第一视角片段及 500 段精选评测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以手为中心的世界模型对人类具身细粒度任务至关重要，其核心在于刻画手部动作如何改变物体以及产生的状态如何随时间持续。然而，针对这类手部中心能力的系统评测方法仍不成熟，缺少统一、可比、跨具身形态的评估基准。

Method: 构建 HandWorldBench 统一基准：(1) 设计数据策管流水线，从 600 余小时素材中抽取手-物交互片段并进行质量筛选，得到 20,500 段高质量第一视角片段；(2) 人工精选 500 段覆盖七类任务族的评测片段；(3) 定义视觉质量、手部保真度、外观一致性、指令遵循、物理一致性、时序连贯性六维评测体系；(4) 统一协议下对 11 个模型进行基准测试，涵盖人手、仿真灵巧手与真实灵巧手三种形态。

Result: 基准实验显示：更强的手部姿态控制并不能稳定转化为连贯的物体状态演化与状态持续能力，也无法保证稳健的跨具身泛化性能；若干专用手部控制模型在灵巧手任务上反而不及通用文本/图像到视频生成模型。

Conclusion: 手部姿态控制的精度与物体状态建模、跨具身泛化之间存在明显能力断层，现有以手为中心的世界模型尚未实现手-物耦合与状态持续的统一建模。HandWorldBench 为该方向提供了标准化评测手段，并揭示后续研究需重点关注手-物联合动力学与跨具身一致性。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models are opening new possibilities for fine-grained embodied tasks, with hand-object interaction offering a particularly demanding setting. Modeling these interactions involves capturing how hand actions change objects and how the resulting states persist over time. Yet systematic evaluation tailored to these hand-centric capabilities remains underdeveloped. To address this gap, we introduce HandWorldBench, a unified benchmark that systematically evaluates hand-centric world models across human hands, simulated dexterous hands, and real dexterous hands along six dimensions: visual quality, hand fidelity, appearance consistency, instruction following, physical consistency, and temporal coherence. To construct the benchmark, we develop a data curation pipeline that extracts hand-object interaction clips and screens them for quality, yielding 20,500 high-quality egocentric clips from over 600 hours of footage. We then manually select 500 clips covering seven task families for evaluation. Benchmarking eleven models under a unified protocol reveals that stronger hand-pose control does not consistently translate into coherent object-state evolution and persistence or robust cross-embodiment performance, with several specialized hand-control models underperforming general-purpose text/image-to-video generators on dexterous-hand tasks.

</details>


### [171] [Jeveration: System One Models Now Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24738)
*Yuhe Liu,Qi Li,Shihua Zhang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Jeveration：基于冻结视觉-语言模型，通过共享随机状态协调并行预测，实现单次前向生成高质量图像，并统一文生图、图生图与稠密预测三类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续 System One 式（如 Jev）的快速并行预测决策范式，探索其能否在单次前向传播中生成高质量图像；核心难点在于并行预测需收敛到整体一致的图像，而单一提示对应多张合法图像。

Method: 以冻结的视觉-语言模型为基础，复用其视觉-语义知识做整体图像生成；将所有位置以共享随机状态为条件以协调并行预测，保证每次采样不同但内部一致；因直接图像监督下模型易忽略该状态，先学习离散图像码的分布以引导单次生成；在同一架构中统一文生图、图生图和稠密预测，一个 checkpoint 一次前向完成三任务。

Result: 512×512 图像生成耗时 78 ms，比现有统一模型快至 122 倍，同时保持有竞争力的图像质量与稠密预测性能。

Conclusion: System One 式并行预测范式可有效用于单次前向的高质量图像生成与多任务统一，显著提升生成速度并保持质量。

Abstract: System One models such as Jev return decisions through fast, parallel prediction. In this paper, we introduce Jeveration, showing that the same decision paradigm can also generate high-quality images in a single forward pass. Jeveration builds on a frozen vision-language model, repurposing its visual-semantic knowledge for holistic image generation. The key challenge is to maintain global consistency. A single prompt admits many valid images, yet parallel predictions must converge on a coherent whole. We therefore condition all positions on a shared random state to coordinate parallel predictions, so that each draw yields a different but coherent image. Since the model tends to ignore this state under direct image supervision, we first learn a distribution over discrete image codes to guide single-pass generation. Beyond text-to-image generation, we further unify image-to-image generation and dense prediction within the same architecture, so that one checkpoint supports all three tasks, each in one forward pass. Experiments show that Jeveration generates a 512x512 image in 78 ms, up to 122x faster than existing unified models, while maintaining competitive image quality and dense prediction performance.

</details>


### [172] [A$^3$Bench: Compositional Reasoning over Aggressive Interactions in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24762)
*Austin C Baker,Raiyaan Abdullah,Daniel Zeev Yoffe,Shruti Vyas,Yogesh S Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 A³Bench 基准，评测多模态大模型在视频攻击性交互中的"角色识别与动作-参与者绑定"能力；17 个模型普遍失败，规模增大或通用推理模型提升有限，role-graph prompting 可缓解组合推理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暴力/犯罪数据集只判断"是否发生攻击"，不考察模型能否区分施害者、受害者与旁观者，以及能否把动作正确绑定到参与者，这一细粒度角色推理能力尚属空白。

Method: 构建 A³Bench 视频基准，联合评测攻击动作识别、施害者/受害者/旁观者区分及挑战性干扰项下的动作-参与者绑定；对 17 个多模态模型进行基准测试，并按推理复杂度分层分析；提出 role-graph prompting，引导模型对参与者交互进行结构化推理。

Result: 所有模型在细粒度攻击场景理解上表现不佳；随推理复杂度上升性能系统性下降；模型常将施害者与受害者或旁观者混淆；增大模型规模或采用通用推理模型带来的增益有限；role-graph prompting 在组合推理上取得改进。

Conclusion: 可靠的攻击场景理解仍是当前 MLLM 的重大挑战，失败本质是角色-动作关联的系统性缺陷而非粗粒度攻击识别；需发展超越暴力检测的角色感知视频推理方法。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on general video understanding benchmarks, yet their ability to reason about participant roles in aggressive interactions remains largely unexplored. Existing violence and crime datasets primarily evaluate whether aggression occurs, without testing whether models can identify who performed the action, who received it, and how participants are related within the scene. To address this gap, we introduce A$^3$Bench, a benchmark to study aggressive action and association in videos. The benchmark evaluates whether models can jointly recognize aggressive actions, distinguish aggressors from victims and bystanders, and correctly bind actions to participants under challenging distractor settings. We benchmark 17 multimodal models and find that they struggle substantially on fine-grained aggressive-scene understanding. Performance degrades consistently as reasoning complexity increases, with models frequently confusing aggressors with victims or bystanders, revealing a systematic failure in role-action association rather than coarse aggression recognition alone.
